Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The sets variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 136 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 292 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
